Comprehensive experiments on the Harvard, BraTS, and GFP datasets reveal that MIND delivers superior fusion quality, significantly improves downstream brain tumor segmentation accuracy, and enables flexible interactive fusion, holding significant promise for intent-driven intelligent clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [99] [Finding Change in Satellite Archives from Text: How to Combine Before-and-After Images Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28571)
*Simon Roy,Mark Bong,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了双时相卫星影像变化检索中融合模块的八种设计（注意力、状态空间模型Mamba、学习压缩TBF），在LEVIR-CC和Dubai-CC基准上进行了十次随机种子实验。主要发现：（1）免训练的两阶段搜索（廉价差分模型粗筛+注意力精排）可将查询成本降低10-15倍且召回率不下降；（2）Mamba的线性扫描在典型视觉Transformer补丁数量下没有速度优势，受限于内存带宽；（3）TBF压缩可减少2.3倍参数和1.6倍延迟，但过度压缩会丢失变化相关细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务地球观测需要在大规模双时相卫星影像档案中高效检索与自然语言变化描述匹配的图像对。融合模块必须在查询时对大量候选对运行，其速度很大程度上决定了搜索成本，因此需要系统研究如何构建高效且有效的融合模块。

Method: 采用固定CLIP图像编码器和统一训练配方，将八种融合模块设计分为三个家族：注意力机制、状态空间模型（Mamba）、学习压缩（Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF）。在LEVIR-CC和Dubai-CC两个基准上以十次随机种子进行受控对比实验。

Result: （1）免训练的两阶段搜索在LEVIR-CC上达到或超过全融合召回率，同时将查询成本降低10-15倍，在Dubai-CC上R@1/R@5相当；（2）Mamba的线性扫描在L=196补丁数下无速度优势，受内存带宽限制，而注意力能很好地利用并行硬件；（3）TBF压缩减少2.3倍参数和1.6倍延迟，变化相关BLEU-1仅损失0.007，但更激进的压缩会静默丢失变化相关细节。

Conclusion: 两阶段搜索策略是构建成本高效的融合模块的有效途径；状态空间模型在视觉Transformer的典型规模下难以兑现其理论线性复杂度优势；表示压缩虽能在多数指标上保持性能，但需要警惕其隐藏的变化细节丢失问题。

Abstract: Operational Earth observation increasingly calls for answering queries such as ``find the image pairs where a new building appeared.'' This means searching an archive of before-and-after (bi-temporal) satellite image pairs and ranking each pair by how well it matches a natural-language description of the change. The component that performs this match, the fusion module that combines the ``before'' and ``after'' views, must be run at query time across many candidate pairs, so its speed largely sets the cost of every search. We present a controlled comparison of how to build that module. Using one fixed image encoder (a frozen CLIP model) and one training recipe for all variants, we evaluate eight designs drawn from three families: attention, state-space models (Mamba), and learned compression (our Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF). Each design is tested on two benchmarks (LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC) with ten random seeds, so the reported differences are statistically grounded. We outline three findings: first, a training-free two-stage search (a cheap difference model that shortlists candidates, followed by attention fusion that re-ranks them) matches or exceeds full-fusion recall on LEVIR-CC while cutting query cost $10$-$15\times$, with comparable R@1/R@5 on Dubai-CC; second, the linear-time scan of Mamba, attractive on paper, gives no speed benefit at the patch counts typical of vision transformers ($L{=}196$): the scan is limited by memory bandwidth, whereas attention maps cleanly onto parallel hardware; and third, compressing the fused representation (TBF) reduces parameters by $2.3\times$ and latency by $1.6\times$ for a change-only BLEU-1 cost of $0.007$, although more aggressive compression quietly discards change-relevant detail that aggregate metrics fail to reveal.

</details>


### [100] [ROAD: Reciprocal-Objective Alignment of Discriminative Semantics for 3D Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28581)
*Xiao Luo,Mingyang Du,Xin Zhou,Tianrui Feng,Xiwu Chen,Xiaofan Li,Jiangning Zhang,Dingkang Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROAD框架通过将判别式3D基础模型中的丰富先验知识迁移到扩散Transformer中，大幅降低3D生成的训练成本，仅用工业基线Step1X-3D的1.5%训练数据即可取得极具竞争力的生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真3D生成主要依赖扩大模型容量和数据规模，计算成本高昂，且通常从零学习几何结构，忽视已封装的判别式3D基础模型的丰富语义和结构先验。作者认为利用这些判别式模型的3D世界理解能力可显著降低生成成本。

Method: 提出ROAD框架，将判别式先验迁移到扩散Transformer中。为解决生成与判别隐空间之间的语义-结构异质性，引入互惠目标对齐策略，包括：1) 整体语义浓缩（Holistic Semantic Condensing）用于强制全局语义一致性；2) 结构最优对齐（Structural Optimal Alignment），形式化为二分图匹配问题以严格对齐不同隐空间之间的微观几何细节。3D基础模型仅用于训练时的对齐监督，推理时不使用，不增加额外推理成本。

Result: 与工业基线Step1X-3D相比，ROAD仅使用1.5%的训练数据即达到极具竞争力的生成性能，显著降低了训练成本，有效减少了高保真3D生成的计算开销。

Conclusion: 利用判别式3D基础模型的先验知识可以有效降低3D生成模型的训练成本，为高保真3D生成提供了高效且经济的替代范式。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D generation predominantly relies on scaling model capacity and data, which incurs prohibitive computational costs. This paradigm typically requires learning geometry from scratch and overlooks the rich semantic and structural priors already encapsulated in discriminative 3D foundation models. We contend that leveraging the profound understanding of the 3D world possessed by these discriminative models can significantly reduce generative cost. To this end, we propose ROAD, a framework that reduces the training cost of 3D generation by transferring these rich discriminative priors into diffusion transformers. To address the inherent semantic-structural heterogeneity between generative and discriminative latents, we introduce a reciprocal-objective alignment strategy. This method synergizes Holistic Semantic Condensing to enforce global semantic coherence and Structural Optimal Alignment, which is formulated as a bipartite matching problem to rigorously align microscopic geometric details between disparate latent spaces. The 3D foundation model is only used for training-time supervision of alignment and is not used at inference, incurring no additional inference cost. Compared with the industrial baseline Step1X-3D, the proposed ROAD achieves highly competitive generation performance with only 1.5% of the training data and significantly reduces training costs, effectively reducing the computational overhead of high-fidelity 3D generation. Code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/ROAD.

</details>


### [101] [MixFrag: Fragility-Guided Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28589)
*Md. Mehrab Hossain Opi,Robiul Islam Ryad,Md. Umar Faruk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixFrag是一个基于脆弱性引导的混合精度后训练量化框架，通过KL散度估计组件级量化脆弱性，并将比特分配建模为多选择背包问题，在ImageNet-1K分类和COCO检测/分割任务上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法对所有Transformer组件采用统一比特宽度，忽略了不同组件对量化的敏感度差异，导致精度分配效率低下。

Method: 1) 通过测量全精度与孤立量化输出分布之间的KL散度来估计组件级量化脆弱性；2) 将比特分配形式化为多选择背包问题（MCKP），在目标比特预算下实现自适应的逐层精度分配。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K多个ViT架构上实现竞争性分类性能；在COCO目标检测和实例分割上达到SOTA，在MP3/MP3设置下相较此前最佳方法提升最高9.6 AP。

Conclusion: 脆弱性度量与学习到的比特分配高度相关，验证了MixFrag作为ViT混合精度后训练量化框架的有效性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has emerged as an effective solution for deploying Vision Transformers (ViTs) on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTQ methods typically employ uniform bit-widths across transformer components, overlooking their heterogeneous sensitivity to quantization and leading to inefficient precision allocation. In this paper, we propose {MixFrag, a fragility-guided mixed-precision PTQ framework for Vision Transformers. MixFrag first estimates component-level quantization fragility by measuring the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between full-precision and isolated quantized output distributions using a small calibration set. It then formulates bit allocation as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), enabling adaptive layer-wise precision assignment under a target bit budget. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-1K across multiple Vision Transformer architectures demonstrate that MixFrag achieves competitive classification performance under practical mixed-precision settings. Furthermore, evaluations on COCO object detection and instance segmentation show that MixFrag achieves state-of-the-art performance among existing mixed-precision PTQ methods, improving the previous best method by up to 9.6 AP under the challenging MP3/MP3 setting. Additional analyses validate the proposed fragility metric and demonstrate its strong correlation with the learned bit allocation. These results establish MixFrag as an effective framework for mixed-precision post-training quantization of Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [102] [VAD: Attributing Visual Evidence for Target Reconstruction in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28590)
*Kangning Zhang,Yixing Li,Shuai Shao,Qingyao Li,Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Jianghao Lin,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉归因蒸馏（VAD），通过反事实目标重构估计教师修正中由视觉证据支持的部分，并以此作为主要监督信号，在六个细粒度视觉基准上超越直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态on-policy蒸馏（OPD）虽然能通过特权视图教师监督学生生成轨迹，但其下一token修正信号是源混合的，混杂了视觉信号、语言先验和教师特定效应。关键在于估计哪些修正是由视觉证据支持的，而非仅仅决定在何处或如何强地蒸馏。

Method: VAD是一个反事实目标重构算法。在每个学生生成前缀处，用同一固定教师在证据存在和移除两种情况下进行评估，计算中心化对数概率的变化作为ut，即视觉证据方向的符号代理。将原始修正投影到该代理上，得到干预对齐分量和代理未解释残差，然后从对齐分量重构学生锚定的目标。训练时以重构目标为主要监督信号，特权视图教师仅作为弱正则化器。

Result: 在4B和9B规模的六个细粒度视觉基准上，VAD优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权。Token级和受控目标分析表明，代理对齐分量富含任务相关的视觉修正，并产生更强的目标偏移，尤其在证据驳斥错误答案时效果显著。

Conclusion: 反事实目标重构是源混合监督的有效替代方案，能够从教师修正中提取视觉可归因部分，提升多模态on-policy蒸馏的效果。

Abstract: Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) transfers fine-grained visual knowledge by supervising student-generated trajectories with a privileged-view teacher. Yet its next-token corrections are source-mixed, combining visual signals with linguistic priors and teacher-specific effects. The key challenge is to estimate which corrections are supported by visual evidence, not merely where or how strongly to distill. We introduce Visual Attribution Distillation (VAD), a counterfactual target-reconstruction algorithm that estimates the visually attributable part of a teacher correction. At each student-generated prefix, VAD evaluates the same fixed teacher with the relevant evidence present and removed. The corresponding change in centered log-probabilities defines ut, a signed proxy for the visual evidence direction that estimates how revealing the evidence supports or refutes candidate tokens. VAD projects the original correction onto this proxy to obtain an intervention-aligned component and a proxy-unexplained residual, then reconstructs a student-anchored target from the former. During training, this reconstructed target supplies the primary supervision signal, while the privileged teacher contributes a weak regularizer. Across six fine-grained visual benchmarks at 4B and 9B scales, VAD outperforms direct privileged-view distillation and visual-advantage weighting. Token- level and controlled-target analyses show that the proxy-aligned component is enriched in task-relevant visual corrections and yields stronger target shifts, especially when evidence refutes a mistaken answer. These results support counterfactual target reconstruction as an effective alternative to source-mixed supervision.

</details>


### [103] [Beacon: Knowing When and How to Perform Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28595)
*Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Letian Cheng,Zhuoran Zhang,Yan He,Yuqi Tang,Qi Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Ruizhe Chen,Tianrun Xu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Haotian Wang,Xianghua Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Beacon，一种面向智能体视觉推理的多模态大语言模型，通过两个关键维度（模式自适应性MA和工具效应TE）重新审视工具使用问题，并利用必要性感知自适应奖励和提示引导的能力扩展机制在强化学习阶段实现更优的工具调用与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体视觉推理模型的核心目标应是提升多模态大语言模型在复杂任务上的成功率，而非仅仅装备复杂但低效的推理范式。作者通过实证分析发现：现有模型在模式自适应性（何时真正需要工具）上表现有限，且工具使用带来的收益在很大程度上被简单样例上引入的额外错误所抵消。这揭示了工具使用的两个关键问题——不必要的工具调用浪费计算资源，以及工具在简单问题上可能引入负面效果。

Method: Beacon提出了两种核心机制：(1) Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward（必要性感知自适应奖励），鼓励模型基于任务实际需要自适应地选择是否调用工具，避免不必要的计算开销；(2) Hint-Guided Capability Expansion（提示引导的能力扩展机制），在强化学习阶段强化模型在最具挑战性问题上的工具使用能力，将工具的作用集中于真正需要扩展能力的场景。整个框架在强化学习阶段联合优化这两个机制。

Result: 在多个多样化基准测试上的大量实验表明，Beacon在整体性能上优于现有方法，同时在模式自适应性（MA）和工具效应（TE）两个维度上均取得了显著的改进，证明其在避免不必要工具调用的同时，能在困难问题上获得真正由工具带来的性能增益。

Conclusion: 智能体视觉推理应同时关注何时使用工具（模式自适应性）以及工具实际带来的效果（工具效应）。Beacon通过强化学习阶段的自适应奖励和针对困难问题的能力扩展机制，有效解决了现有模型在这两个维度上的不足，实现了更高效且更有效的工具使用。

Abstract: The fundamental goal of agentic visual reasoning is to improve the success rate of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on complex tasks, rather than merely equipping them with a sophisticated yet inefficient reasoning paradigm. In this work, we rethink agentic visual reasoning through two key dimensions of tool use: Mode Adaptiveness (MA) and Tool Effect (TE). Mode Adaptiveness characterizes whether an MLLM can recognize when tools are truly necessary and invoke them accordingly, thereby avoiding unnecessary computational overhead while improving performance on challenging problems that require tool assistance. Tool Effect characterizes the actual impact of tool use: tools should extend the model's capabilities on problems unsolvable through text-only reasoning, while avoiding additional errors on problems that the model can already solve without tools. We conduct a comprehensive analysis to quantify these two properties and empirically reveal that existing agentic visual reasoning models exhibit limited Mode Adaptiveness, while the gains produced by tool use on hard examples are largely offset by the harm introduced on easy examples that the models can already solve. Motivated by these observations, we propose Beacon, a novel agentic visual reasoning model that achieves stronger overall performance, improved Mode Adaptiveness, and genuine tool-induced performance gains. At the core of Beacon are the Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward and the Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism in the reinforcement learning stage, which respectively encourage adaptive tool invocation based on task necessity and strengthen the model's tool-use capability on the most challenging problems. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the strong overall performance of Beacon and its substantial improvements in both Mode Adaptiveness and Tool Effect.

</details>


### [104] [Chimera: Designing and Chinchilla-Scaling Hybrid Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28611)
*Chongjian Ge,Hanwen Jiang,Tianyu Wang,Jiuxiang Gu,Yiran Xu,Ziwen Chen,Shaoteng Liu,Jing Shi,Yicong Hong,Zefan Cai,Hailin Jin,Hao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Chimera是一种混合视觉扩散骨干架构，通过Kim Delta Attention（O(N)复杂度）、Multi-head Latent Attention和模态感知短卷积的组合，以及稀疏MoE层，实现了比全注意力基线高1.7x~7.3x的计算效率。配合HeteroP模块级缩放方案和Chinchilla风格缩放定律，训练的11B（2B激活参数）模型可零样本从5秒训练片段外推到30秒视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成日益需要高分辨率图像、长视频和多模态上下文，而全注意力的二次方计算成本变得不可承受，亟需一种既能高效处理长上下文、又具有可扩展性的混合架构。

Method: 1) 架构设计：将文本、图像和视频token统一为单一光栅顺序流（无需位置编码），结合KDA（长上下文状态跟踪，O(N)复杂度）、交错MLA（全局直接交互）、模态感知短卷积（局部时空上下文）以及稀疏MoE层（扩展容量控制激活计算）。2) 缩放方案：提出HeteroP，一种按模块逐层传递超参数的方案，根据各张量的函数扇入和模型深度在宽度和深度间迁移超参数。3) 缩放定律：拟合Chinchilla风格的计算最优定律，确定激活模型大小、训练token数和图像-视频数据比例的最优配置。

Result: 1) 预训练扩散损失方面，稠密骨干比匹配的全注意力Wan-2.1 2B基线计算效率高1.7x，完整系统达7.3x；2) 无需长度特定微调，零样本从5秒训练片段外推到30秒视频，最后5秒仅有6.5% FID退化；3) 缩放定律显示，图像预训练的计算最优分配在激活模型大小和训练token数间几乎对半，而视频预训练在更高预算下略微偏向模型大小。

Conclusion: Chimera验证了混合扩散架构在长上下文视觉生成中的可行性和高效性，HeteroP和缩放定律为设计可扩展的长上下文扩散架构奠定了基础，是该领域的重要进展。

Abstract: Visual generation increasingly requires high-resolution images, long videos, and multimodal context, making the quadratic cost of full attention prohibitive. We introduce Chimera, a hybrid visual diffusion backbone with a principled scaling recipe. Chimera processes text, image, and video tokens in one raster-ordered stream without positional embeddings. It combines Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) for long-context state tracking with O(N) complexity, interleaved Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) for direct global interaction, and modality-aware short convolutions for local spatiotemporal context. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers expand capacity while controlling activated compute. To scale this heterogeneous architecture, we introduce HeteroP, a module-wise scheme that transfers hyperparameters across width and depth according to each tensor's functional fan-in and model depth. HeteroP yields a consistently tuned family used to fit Chinchilla-style compute-optimal laws for activated model size, training-token count, and image-video data ratio. Guided by these laws, we train an 11B-parameter Chimera with 2B activated parameters. Experiments show three results. First, measured by pretraining diffusion loss, the dense backbone is 1.7x as compute-efficient as a matched full-attention Wan-2.1 2B baseline, while the complete system reaches 7.3x. Second, without length-specific fine-tuning, Chimera extrapolates zero-shot from 5-second training clips to 30-second videos, with only 6.5% FID degradation in the last five seconds. Third, the fitted laws show that compute-optimal image pretraining divides compute nearly evenly between activated model size and training-token count, whereas video pretraining modestly favors model size at higher budgets. These results establish a foundation for designing and scaling efficient long-context diffusion architectures.

</details>


### [105] [PhiZero: A World Model Built Around Physical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28624)
*Shuyao Shang,Yuqi Wang,Ruopeng Gao,Xu Chen,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhiZero，一个基于"物理语言"（physical language）的物理世界模型。物理语言是一种紧凑的离散世界状态转换表示，PhiZero采用"先推理后渲染"（reason-then-render）范式，先从视频中自监督学习物理语言，用于显式推理物理世界的演化，再将其渲染为视频，在生成和理解基准上验证了其建模物理一致世界演化的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理世界模型通常直接在像素空间预测未来视频，将底层世界动态隐含在高维视觉预测器中，缺乏显式推理能力。人类能够从视觉经验中抽象出预测结构，并以自然语言组织用于显式推理。受此启发，作者希望构建一个能够显式推理物理世界演化的世界模型，而非仅做隐式的像素级预测。

Method: PhiZero首先从非受限（in-the-wild）视频中通过自监督学习获得物理语言（physical language）——一种紧凑的离散世界状态转换表示。随后采用reason-then-render两阶段范式：第一阶段根据当前世界状态推理出未来的物理语言序列（即世界演化）；第二阶段将推理出的状态转换渲染为视频。

Result: 在生成和理解基准上的大量实验验证了PhiZero能够建模物理一致的世界演化。此外，PhiZero还展现了在逼真交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件仿真以及零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过引入物理语言这一显式、紧凑的离散世界状态表示，并使用reason-then-render范式，PhiZero证明了将抽象显式推理与视觉渲染解耦是构建物理一致世界模型的有效途径，为可解释、可交互的物理世界建模提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce PhiZero, a physical world model built around physical language, a compact discrete representation of world-state transitions. Existing physical world models typically predict future videos directly in pixel space, leaving the underlying world dynamics implicit within high-dimensional visual predictors. Motivated by humans' ability to abstract predictive structure from visual experience and organize it in natural language for explicit reasoning, we learn physical language from in-the-wild videos through self-supervision and use it to explicitly reason about how the physical world evolves. Accordingly, PhiZero adopts a reason-then-render paradigm: it first infers future world evolution as a physical-language sequence and then renders the inferred transitions into videos. Extensive experiments across generation and understanding benchmarks validate the ability of PhiZero to model physically coherent world evolution. We further show its potential for realistic and interactive world modeling, fine-grained action-conditioned simulation, and zero-shot motion transfer.

</details>


### [106] [ACE-Data-0: Human-Centric Ambient Capture as Embodied Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28625)
*Yukang Cao,Haozhe Xie,Beichen Wen,Runmao Yao,Yinghao Liu,Yue Huang,Zhichao Liao,Yunxiang Wang,Haiheng Liu,Xingshun Tian,Dawei Su,Long Zhuo,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE（Ambient Capture Engine），一种以人为中心的数据引擎，将真实家庭环境转化为空间校准、时间同步的录制工作室，在桌面和房间两个尺度上同步采集第一人称/多视角视频、全身及手部运动、物体几何与轨迹、音频和触觉信号，并构建了包含150小时、1700万帧、75000个交互片段的大规模多模态数据集ACE-Data-0，同时引入从信号到场景组件再到交互的层级基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能面临根本性的数据瓶颈：现有数据集在不同视角、模态或空间尺度上割裂了体验，导致完整的感知-动作闭环只能被部分观测。模型需要捕捉第一人称感知、全身运动、灵巧操作、物体状态、声音和触觉在人类追求目标过程中如何协同演化，但当前数据资源无法支持这一点。

Method: ACE包含两个互补的采集尺度：桌面级配置解析手-物操作，房间级配置捕捉全身运动、移动和家庭交互。它同步记录第一人称与多视角第三人称视频、全身与手部关节运动、物体几何与6-DoF轨迹、音频和触觉信号作为统一多感知流。由此构建ACE-Data-0数据集，包含150小时、1700万帧、200个任务类别、50名参与者、2个环境、共75000个交互片段。数据采集采用目标级而非逐步指令，保留自然行为多样性。此外引入层级基准，从信号到场景组件再到交互递进评估。

Result: 对最先进方法的评估揭示了在接触、遮挡、自主运动和长时间跨度的挑战下存在显著性能差距。ACE-Data-0提供了同步的人体演示，包含对齐的感知、运动学和接触监督，为模仿学习、世界模型、视觉-语言-动作系统和具身AI提供了可扩展基础。

Conclusion: ACE通过统一的多模态同步采集方案解决了具身智能数据碎片化问题，ACE-Data-0作为大规模高质量数据资产，为训练能够感知并与物理世界交互的具身智能模型奠定了规模化基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence faces a fundamental data bottleneck. Models must capture how first-person perception, whole-body motion, dexterous manipulation, object state, sound, and touch evolve together as humans pursue goals over time. Existing datasets fragment this experience across viewpoints, modalities, or spatial scales, leaving the full perception-action loop only partially observed. We introduce the Ambient Capture Engine (ACE), a human-centric data engine that transforms real home environments into spatially calibrated, temporally synchronized recording studios. ACE operates at two complementary scales: a table-scale configuration resolves hand-object manipulation, while a room-scale configuration captures whole-body motion, locomotion, and interactions across a furnished home. ACE records egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, full-body and articulated hand motion, object geometry and 6-DoF trajectories, audio, and tactile signals as a unified multisensory stream. Using ACE, we build ACE-Data-0, comprising 150 hours and 17M video frames across 200 task categories, performed by 50 participants in 2 environments, for a total of 75,000 interaction episodes. The dataset spans atomic manipulation, long-horizon chains of household activities, and human-scene interaction, while preserving natural behavioral variation through goal-level rather than step-by-step instructions. We further introduce a hierarchical benchmark that progresses from signals to scene components and then to interactions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art methods expose substantial gaps under contact, occlusion, egomotion, and long temporal horizons. ACE-Data-0 provides synchronized human demonstrations with aligned perceptual, kinematic, and contact supervision, offering a scalable foundation for imitation learning, world models, vision-language-action systems, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [107] [ReToken: One Token to Improve Vision-Language Models for Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28627)
*Yao Xiao,Reuben Tan,Zhen Zhu,Yuqun Wu,Jianfeng Gao,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReToken提出一个可学习的检索标记（learnable embedding），作为显式检索目标从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选择与查询相关的稀疏视觉token，以解决长视觉上下文中视觉语言模型性能下降和GPU内存不足的问题。仅在小规模图像QA数据集上训练，即在各图像和视频基准上取得一致的性能提升，且训练与长视频推理可在单张H100上完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文对视觉语言模型构成挑战：随着干扰物数量增加，模型性能显著下降；同时一次性处理所有token在GPU内存限制下计算上不可行。现有方法缺乏高效且可扩展的机制来在长上下文中筛选查询相关视觉信息。

Method: ReToken将单个可学习的embedding训练为显式检索目标，从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选择稀疏的、与查询相关的视觉token子集，从而绕过全量计算。仅需在小规模图像QA数据集上进行轻量训练。

Result: 在Visual Haystacks上，ReToken使Qwen3VL-8B提升13.4个百分点、InternVL3.5提升12.4个百分点（相对提升超20%）；在LVBench上以零样本方式迁移到长视频，为Qwen3VL-8B带来8.0个百分点的增益。轻量设计使训练和长视频推理可在单张H100上完成。

Conclusion: ReToken证明了单一可学习检索嵌入作为显式检索目标即可高效实现长视觉上下文的稀疏token选择，兼顾性能提升与计算可行性，并在跨域（图像到视频）场景中具备良好的零样本迁移能力，为长视觉理解提供了一种简洁而实用的方案。

Abstract: Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at: https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

### [108] [Toward Multi-Modal Deep Learning for Pulmonary Disease Classification: A Texture-Based Machine Learning Pilot Study on Public Chest X-Ray Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27286)
*Yogisri Pujitha Chinthoti*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项试点研究，使用经典的纹理和梯度特征（HOG和GLCM）结合传统分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、SVM）对胸部X光片进行肺部疾病自动分类，重点区分COVID-19与其他肺炎类型。在COVID-19图像数据集的668张X光片上，患者级别的5折分层交叉验证下获得75.4%的最佳准确率和0.755的AUC，略优于71.6%的多数类基线。研究结果用于激励和规划未来多模态深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光片的肺部疾病自动分类是医学影像中广泛研究的机器学习应用。本文旨在通过经典特征表示方法进行试点研究，评估其区分COVID-19与其他肺炎类型的有效性，并为未来更复杂的多模态深度学习架构提供依据和方向。

Method: 使用直方图定向梯度（HOG）和灰度共生矩阵（GLCM）纹理描述符作为特征表示，结合经典分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、支持向量机）。采用患者级别5折分层交叉验证以防止数据泄漏，使用公开的COVID-19图像数据收集（408名患者的668张后前位/前后位X光片）。

Result: 最佳平均准确率为75.4%，AUC为0.755，适度超过71.6%的多数类基线。结果被透明报告，并包含其局限性说明。

Conclusion: 经典特征方法仅能适度超越基线，结果有限。作者据此提出未来方向：结合卷积和Transformer编码器的多模态深度学习架构，涵盖多种影像模态，但需获取更大规模、多机构、伦理来源的数据集作为支撑。

Abstract: Automated classification of pulmonary disease from chest radiographs is a widely studied application of machine learning in medical imaging. This paper presents a pilot study evaluating classical texture- and gradient-based feature representations for distinguishing COVID-19 from other forms of pneumonia using the publicly available COVID-19 Image Data Collection (668 posteroanterior/anteroposterior radiographs from 408 patients). Using histogram of oriented gradients (HOG) and gray-level co-occurrence matrix (GLCM) texture descriptors with classical classifiers (logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine), evaluated under patient-level 5-fold stratified cross-validation to prevent data leakage, we obtain a best mean accuracy of 75.4% and AUC of 0.755, modestly exceeding the 71.6% majority-class baseline. We report these results transparently, including their limitations, and use them to motivate and scope a proposed multi-modal deep learning architecture -- combining convolutional and transformer-based encoders across imaging modalities -- as a direction for future work requiring access to larger, multi-institutional, ethically sourced datasets.

</details>


### [109] [Endo-NeRF++: Uncertainty-Aware Neural Rendering with Multi-Resolution Hash Encoding for Dynamic Surgical Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27825)
*Gousia Habib,Laura Ruotsalainen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出Endo-NeRF++，一种面向动态手术场景重建的神经渲染框架，通过多分辨率哈希网格编码、时序特征融合和不确定性引导的自适应采样，在EndoNeRF基础上提升重建精度与时序一致性，PSNR最高提升1.22 dB，SSIM提升5.3%，LPIPS降低55.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助微创手术中的动态手术场景重建至关重要，但由于组织变形、遮挡、镜面反射和视角受限等因素，这一任务仍然面临巨大挑战。现有的EndoNeRF等神经渲染方法在应对这些复杂情况时效果有限，需要更强大的表征与采样策略。

Method: 该框架在EndoNeRF基础上引入三大改进：（1）多分辨率哈希网格编码，同时捕获场景的粗粒度与细粒度解剖细节；（2）时序特征融合，在组织变形和手术器械遮挡期间保证重建的稳定性；（3）不确定性引导的自适应采样，将更多采样点分配到不确定区域，以提升渲染质量和几何一致性。

Result: 在机器人手术视频序列上的实验表明，相比EndoNeRF基线，不确定性引导的自适应采样使PSNR最高提升1.22 dB（4.3%），SSIM最高提升5.3%，LPIPS最高降低55.1%。

Conclusion: Endo-NeRF++通过结合多分辨率哈希网格、时序特征融合与不确定性感知采样，有效改善了动态内窥镜手术场景的重建精度和时序稳定性，为手术场景神经渲染提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic surgical scenes is crucial for robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery; however, it continues to be difficult because of tissue deformation, occlusions, specular reflections, and restricted viewpoints. In this study, we introduce Endo-NeRF++, a neural rendering framework that accounts for uncertainty in the reconstruction of dynamic surgical scenes. Expanding on EndoNeRF, the suggested approach incorporates multi-resolution hash-grid encoding, temporal feature merging, and uncertainty-informed adaptive sampling to enhance reconstruction accuracy and temporal coherence in deformable endoscopic scenes.The multi-resolution hash-grid representation within the framework effectively captures both coarse and fine anatomical details, while temporal feature blending ensures stable reconstruction during tissue deformation and surgical tool occlusions. Additionally, uncertainty-driven adaptive sampling assigns more samples to uncertain areas to enhance rendering quality and geometric coherence. Experiments on robotic surgical video sequences demonstrate that the proposed uncertainty-guided adaptive sampling improves PSNR by up to 1.22\,dB (4.3\%), increases SSIM by up to 5.3\%, and reduces LPIPS by up to 55.1\% compared with the EndoNeRF baseline.

</details>


### [110] [TSOG: A Format For Temporally And Spatially Ordered Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28049)
*Shady Gmira,Evangelos Alexiou,Emmanouil Potetsianakis,Emmanuel Thomas*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出TSOG（Temporally and Spatially Ordered Gaussians），一种高效的4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）内容表示格式，将SOG框架扩展至时间域，引入时间线属性和时空属性参数化。相比PLYs序列和FreeTimeGS基线，TSOG实现了超过90%的文件大小缩减，PSNR差异在-0.42至+0.85 dB之间，在保证极小质量损失的同时大幅提升动态场景的存储和传输效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）在动态场景表示方面表现出色，但其原始数据（如PLYs序列）文件体积巨大，不利于存储和传输。现有格式缺乏对4DGS内容的有效压缩机制，亟需一种既能大幅缩小文件体积、又能保持渲染质量的高效表示格式。

Method: TSOG将SOG框架扩展至时间域，具体方法包括：（1）为每个高斯引入时间线（timeline）属性；（2）对几何和外观属性进行时间参数化；（3）为每个高斯分配唯一索引，将属性值编码为与索引对齐的图像数据（lossy格式）。TSOG是模型无关的，兼容离散和连续两种4DGS表示。

Result: 以PLYs序列（简易基线）和FreeTimeGS（SOTA基线）作为对比，TSOG实现超过90%的文件大小缩减，PSNR差异范围为-0.42至+0.85 dB，展示了大文件节省与最小质量退化之间的良好平衡。

Conclusion: TSOG实现了显著的4DGS文件压缩率（>90%），同时保持几乎不变的渲染质量，为下一代4D内容的表示、存储和传输提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: We propose Temporally and Spatially Ordered Gaussians (TSOG), a format for efficient representation of 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) content. TSOG extends the Spatially Ordered Gaussians (SOG) framework to the temporal domain by introducing a timeline attribute and temporal parameterization of geometry and appearance attributes. Similar to SOG, TSOG is a lossy format that assigns each Gaussian a unique index and encodes attribute values as index-aligned image data. TSOG is model-agnostic, extensible, and compatible with both discrete and continuous 4DGS representations. Evaluation using a PLYs sequence and FreeTimeGS as baselines, serving as simplistic and state-of-the-art 4DGS representations respectively, shows file size reductions exceeding 90%, with PSNR differences ranging between -0.42 and +0.85 dB. These results demonstrate substantial file size savings with minimal quality degradation, enabling efficient representation, storage, and delivery of dynamic scenes for next-generation 4D content.

</details>


### [111] [ReGenVC: End-to-End Real-Time Generative Video Coding at Ultra-Low Bitrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28144)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Johnson Wu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ReGenVC是首个端到端生成式视频编解码器，将说话头视频压缩至极低码率（77帧序列约26kB，仅为传统编解码器的约1/10），并通过四步蒸馏扩散Transformer及三项系统优化技术（八GPU统一序列并行、空间分割VAE、三级重叠流水线），在8-GPU节点上实现24fps实时解码，支持实时浏览器直播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频编解码器能利用强生成先验在极低码率下保持清晰重建，但其核心障碍在于解码器延迟过高——多步采样配合Transformer和VAE组件对交互式使用而言太慢。传统编解码器（x264/x265）在超低码率下会产生块状伪影，亟需一个既具备极低码率又支持实时解码的方案。

Method: 编码端将源片段压缩为紧凑比特流（包含神经压缩的首帧、每帧姿态关键点及元数据，77帧约26kB）；解码端采用四步蒸馏扩散Transformer，基于传输的姿态和参考帧重建视频。为加速解码，采用三项模型保留型系统技术：(i)八GPU统一序列并行（Ulysses & Ring），(ii)空间分割VAE，(iii)三级重叠流水线，并建立分析时序模型刻画实时可行性区域。混合CPU-GPU部署将编码器及一次性条件编码器卸载至CPU，显著降低GPU内存峰值。

Result: 在8-GPU节点上，系统维持24fps输出（25帧窗口972ms，在1000ms预算内），实现无帧下溢的实时浏览器流。码率约为传统编解码器的1/10（约26kB对比250-280kB），同时重建保持清晰无伪影。混合CPU-GPU部署将每GPU内存峰值从21.1GB降至约7.7GB。

Conclusion: ReGenVC是首个将超低码率编码与实时解码结合在8-GPU系统上的端到端生成式视频编解码器，验证了生成式先验结合系统优化可将解码延迟降至实时水平，为生成式视频编解码器的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present ReGenVC, an end-to-end generative video codec that compresses talking-head video to an ultra-low bitrate and decodes it in real time. The encoder reduces a source clip to a compact bitstream -- a neurally compressed first frame, per-frame pose keypoints, and metadata -- totaling about 26 kB for a 77-frame sequence. The decoder is a four-step distilled diffusion transformer that reconstructs the video conditioned on the transmitted pose and reference frame. Compared with x264/x265, ReGenVC reduces the bitrate to roughly one tenth of that required by traditional codecs (about 26 kB vs. 250--280 kB for essentially artifact-free reconstruction); at a matched ultra-low bitrate, conventional codecs collapse into blocking artifacts while ReGenVC stays sharp by exploiting a strong generative prior. The central obstacle to deploying such a codec is decoder latency: multi-step sampling with transformer and VAE components is too slow for interactive use. We make the decoder real-time through four-step distillation and three model-preserving system techniques: (i) eight-GPU unified sequence parallelism (Ulysses & Ring), (ii) a spatially-split VAE, and (iii) a three-stage overlapped pipeline; an analytical timing model characterizes the real-time feasibility region. On an 8-GPU node, the system sustains 24 fps output (972 ms per 25-frame window, within the 1000 ms budget), enabling a live browser stream without observed frame underruns. A hybrid CPU-GPU deployment further runs the encoder on the CPU at 24 fps and offloads the decoder-side one-shot conditioning encoders to the CPU, reducing the per-GPU memory peak from 21.1 GB to about 7.7 GB. To our knowledge, ReGenVC is the first end-to-end generative video codec to combine ultra-low-bitrate encoding with real-time decoding on an 8-GPU system.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [It's Not Just More Demos: Counterfactual Action Sensitivity Coverage for Data-Efficient Robust Robot Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27261)
*Giovanni D'urso,Kaushik Roy,Nicholas Lawrance,Brendan Tidd*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CFNBC框架，通过生成配对的干净/干扰观测并测量策略的动作漂移（action drift），无需在线执行或成功标签即可离线选择针对性修复样本，以恢复模仿学习策略在视觉干扰下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动模仿学习策略对环境干扰（光照、干扰物、颜色变化等）非常脆弱，即使微小的、不改变任务语义的视觉变化也会导致策略性能显著退化。虽然增加数据多样性可以提升鲁棒性，但难以判断哪些额外的演示对特定已训练策略最有信息量，缺乏一个策略专用的数据选择信号。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Nuisance Behaviour Cloning (CFNBC)，一种离线数据选择框架。核心思想是：从干净演示训练的标称策略出发，生成配对的干净与干扰观测（保持专家动作不变），测量策略在干扰下的动作漂移（action drift）——即预测动作的变化量。动作漂移作为策略特定的敏感性信号，用于从更大候选池中选择紧凑且响应多样的修复集合。整个过程无需回滚成功标签或在线策略执行。

Result: 在MuJoCo双机械臂方块搬运和SimplerEnv方块堆叠任务中验证：动作漂移与干扰引起的失败强相关；基于响应引导的修复仅用20-30个候选样本就在性能上大幅超越随机选择（相同预算），并接近使用更大随机修复预算的效果。

Conclusion: 支持一种数据中心的鲁棒性修复观点：最有价值的数据并不是数量最多、视觉上最多样或最明显的困难样本，而是能够覆盖当前策略脆弱响应模式（fragile response modes）的示例。

Abstract: Visuomotor imitation learning has demonstrated success for manipulation tasks. However, the trained policies remain brittle to visual `nuisances', with even minor task-preserving variations such as lighting, distractions or changes in colour result in heavy degradation of the trained policy's performance. While increasing data diversity can improve robustness, it is unclear which additional demonstrations are informative for a particular trained policy. We propose Counterfactual Nuisance Behaviour Cloning (CFNBC), an offline data-selection framework for targeted robustness repair. Starting from a nominal policy trained on `clean' demonstrations, CFNBC generates paired clean and nuisance observations that preserve the expert action, then measures \emph{action drift}: the change in the policy's predicted action under a nuisance that should not alter the desired behaviour. This provides a policy-specific sensitivity signal for selecting a compact, response-diverse repair set from a larger candidate pool, without requiring rollout success labels or online policy execution. We show in MuJoCo bimanual cube transfer and SimplerEnv cube stacking that action drift correlates with nuisance-induced failure, and that response-guided repair with only $20$--$30$ selected candidates substantially outperforms matched-budget random selection while approaching the performance of much larger random repair budgets. These results support a data-centric view of robustness repair: the most useful data are not necessarily the most numerous, visually diverse, or obviously difficult, but the examples that cover fragile response modes of the current policy.

</details>


### [113] [Simulation of Surgical Suturing Using Position-Based Dynamics and the Material Point Method for Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27494)
*Tleukhan Mussin,Yafei Ou,Mahdi Tavakoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合位置动力学（PBD）模拟缝合线和物质点法（MPM）模拟软组织的缝合仿真环境，通过双向接触耦合实现缝线-组织交互，并针对GPU进行了并行优化，同时提供了强化学习环境用于自主缝合子任务训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人研究对高性能模拟器需求强烈，而手术机器人仿真的独特挑战在于需同时建模刚性器械、软组织和流体等多样对象。现有研究大多单独模拟缝线或软组织，很少考虑完整软组织缝合场景，特别是缝线插入过程中与可变形组织的接触交互。

Method: 采用PBD建模缝线、MPM建模软组织，提出PBD缝线与MPM软组织之间的接触耦合方法，考虑摩擦力和阻力以实现视觉上合理的缝线-组织交互；仿真器通过多CUDA流并行场景进行GPU优化；基于ML-Agents构建强化学习环境，涵盖针插入、驱动和拔出等自主缝合子任务。

Result: 在ML-Agents框架下训练的RL智能体表现出稳定的学习过程，在最严格的距离阈值下，针插入和拔出任务分别达到80%和68%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该仿真环境能够有效模拟完整的软组织缝合场景，支持强化学习训练，为自主手术缝合机器人提供了可行的模拟训练平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in robotics research have created a strong demand for high-performance simulators. Surgical robotics simulation faces unique challenges due to the need to model diverse objects, such as rigid instruments, soft tissue, and fluids. While many studies simulate sutures or soft tissue independently, only a few have considered the complete soft-tissue suturing scenario, including the contact between sutures and deformable tissue during suture insertion. Building on previous work, this paper presents a novel suturing simulation environment using sutures modelled by position-based dynamics (PBD) and soft bodies modelled by the material point method (MPM) while considering two-way contact with frictional and drag forces. We introduce a contact coupling method between the PBD suture and the MPM soft tissue, enabling visually plausible suture-tissue interactions. The simulator is optimized for GPU execution with parallel scenes using multiple CUDA streams, and we present a Reinforcement Learning (RL) environment for autonomous suturing sub-tasks, including needle insertion, driving, and extraction. Using ML-Agents, RL agents trained in the simulator show stable learning and achieve 80% and 68% success rates in needle insertion and extraction, respectively, under the strictest distance threshold.

</details>


### [114] [Corrigible Assistance in One Round: Pragmatic-Pedagogic Best Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27508)
*Elle Lazarski,Jaime Fernández Fisac*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文识别出一类辅助游戏（assistance games），在该类游戏中，通过语用-教学推理（pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning）能在单步时间内消除目标不确定性，使得完整时域博弈可通过可处理的best-response过程精确求解。文章进一步对比了主流逆向最优控制在推理上存在的天花板，并验证了所提方法在协作积木搭建任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作中，机器人需要从有限的观察中推断人类目标并提供有效辅助，这通常被形式化为辅助游戏。然而，由于精确求解需要在POMDP中进行规划，在线计算最优策略在一般情况下是难以处理的（intractable），因此需要寻找可精确求解的特特殊子类。

Method: 作者识别了一类特殊的辅助游戏，其中语用-教学推理能在单个时间步内解决目标不确定性，从而将全时域博弈化简为可解的best-response过程。该方法利用人类在教学中表现出的语用行为（pragmatic behavior），使得机器人能够立即从看似仅在任务执行层面等价的动作中推断出真实目标。

Result: 研究表明，在此类辅助游戏中，主流逆向最优控制方法存在推理上限（inference ceiling），会阻碍人机对齐；而语用-教学推理能通过动作消除目标歧义，突破这一障碍。作者在简单的协作积木搭建示例上验证了理论结果和所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 语用-教学推理为特定类别的辅助游戏提供了一种精确且可计算的最优策略求解途径，克服了逆向最优控制在目标推断上的固有局限，为人机对齐提供了一种更有效的理论框架。

Abstract: Assistance games formalize human-robot collaboration under asymmetric information: the human knows the goal, while the robot must infer it from observation and interaction in order to assist effectively. In general, computing optimal assistance game strategies online is intractable, since exact solutions require planning in a POMDP. We identify a class of assistance games in which pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning resolves goal uncertainty in a single time step, rendering the full-horizon game exactly solvable by a tractable best-response procedure. Within this class, we show that mainstream inverse optimal control exhibits an inference ceiling that hinders alignment, while pragmatic-pedagogic reasoning overcomes this barrier by immediately disambiguating goals through actions that look equivalent under task execution alone. Finally, we validate our theoretical results and proposed method on a simple collaborative block-building example.

</details>


### [115] [Failure Detection for Surgical Robot Imitation Policies via Flow-Matching World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27511)
*Zhefeng Huang,Yilin Cai,Ankit Patel,Mohammad Hajiha,Brendan Browne,Yue Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoMo-FD是一种基于流匹配世界模型的机器人手术失败检测方法，通过学习正常短时视觉动力学并利用逆传输非符合性得分检测视觉-动作不一致，无需失败演示数据即可实现窗口级失败检测，在dVRK仿真和真实实验中达到96.6%检测率、1.3%误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在自主机器人手术中展现出前景，但手术任务的安全性关键性和环境复杂性使得安全部署充满挑战。失败检测作为安全保障至关重要，然而面临失败数据稀缺、操作动力学高度多变、以及需要在漏检与干扰性误报之间权衡等难题。

Method: FoMo-FD使用动作条件流匹配世界模型学习标称（正常）短时视觉动力学，对观测到的端点潜在表示计算逆传输非符合性得分，从而在窗口级别检测视觉-动作不一致性，无需失败演示。检测阈值通过符合性校准在成功执行数据上获得，可生成任务特定报警，无需假设未来失败类型。

Result: 在da Vinci Research Kit（dVRK）上的四个手术相关操作任务、二十种失败模式的仿真和真实实验评估中，FoMo-FD优于观测级异常检测基线及其世界模型本身的预测误差变体；腕部相机视角表现最强，达到96.6%的失败检测率和1.3%的误报率。

Conclusion: FoMo-FD证明了利用流匹配世界模型学习正常动态并基于逆传输非符合性进行失败检测的有效性，无需失败样本即可实现高检测率低误报率的失败检测，为手术机器人安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning has shown increasing promise for autonomous robotic surgery, yet safe deployment remains challenging due to the safety-critical nature of surgical tasks and the complexity and variability of surgical environments. Failure detection is therefore an essential safeguard, but its development remains difficult due to the challenges of scarce failure data, highly variable manipulation dynamics, and the need to balance missed detections against disruptive false alarms. To address these challenges, we introduce FoMo-FD (Flow-Matching World Model for Failure Detection), a failure detection method that learns nominal short-horizon visual dynamics with an action-conditioned flow-matching world model. FoMo-FD scores the inverse-transport nonconformity of observed endpoint latents, enabling window-level detection of visual-action inconsistencies without requiring failure demonstrations. Detection thresholds are obtained by conformal calibration on successful executions, yielding task-specific alarms without assuming future failure types. We evaluate FoMo-FD on four surgically relevant manipulation tasks with twenty failure modes across simulation and real-world experiments using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). Results show that FoMo-FD outperforms observation-level anomaly baselines and a prediction-error variant of the same world model, with the wrist-camera view achieving the strongest performance, including a 96.6% failure detection rate (FDR) at a 1.3% false alarm rate (FAR).

</details>


### [116] [Cross-Embodiment Transfer via Behavior-Aligned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27549)
*Ajay Sridhar,Jensen Gao,Jonathan Yang,Jean Mercat,Suneel Belkhale,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究行为对齐表征（如物体边界框、语言动作、末端执行器轨迹）在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的作用，以促进跨具身（cross-embodiment）迁移。通过在仿真基准上的系统比较，发现末端执行器轨迹表征对迁移特别有益，表征在更大规模的先验数据集上更有用，且可利用无动作数据，并将仿真到现实的跨具身任务完成进度提升了28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模仿学习依赖跨多种机器人具身的数据集，但实现显著的跨具身迁移仍然具有挑战性。作者希望找到能统一大规模跨具身数据、增强迁移能力的表征方法。

Method: 提出了行为对齐表征（behavior-aligned representations）的概念，即那些在不同具身之间具有不变性、同时对预测机器人动作具有预测力的表征（如物体边界框、语言运动、末端执行器轨迹）。开发了一个基于仿真的基准来评估跨具身迁移，系统比较不同表征类型及其引入方式，并评估其在仿真-到-现实跨具身迁移中的效果。

Result: 末端执行器轨迹（end-effector traces）对迁移特别有帮助；表征在更大的先验数据集下通常更有用；表征可以用于受益于无动作数据；在仿真数据预训练的真实机器人策略上，将任务完成进度提升了28%。

Conclusion: 行为对齐表征，尤其是末端执行器轨迹，是促进VLA模型跨具身迁移的有效手段，能够统一跨具身数据并增强从仿真到现实的迁移性能。

Abstract: Recent progress in large-scale imitation learning for robot manipulation has been driven by leveraging datasets across a wide range of robot embodiments. However, achieving significant cross-embodiment transfer is often still challenging. In this work, we study the role of using behavior-aligned representations (e.g., object bounding boxes, language motions, end-effector traces of robot motion) in vision-language-action (VLA) models to promote cross-embodiment transfer. We hypothesize that by possessing invariances across embodiments while being predictive of robot actions, these representations can help unify large-scale cross-embodiment data to enhance transfer. To assess our hypothesis, we develop a simulation-based benchmark designed to assess transfer with diverse cross-embodiment data to new embodiments. Using this benchmark, we compare different representations and ways of incorporating them. We identify that end-effector traces can be particularly beneficial for transfer, representations are generally more useful with larger prior datasets, and can be used to benefit from action-free data. We also demonstrate that they can enhance sim-to-real cross-embodiment transfer, improving task completion progress of real robot policies pre-trained on simulation data by 28%. We provide videos of our evaluations at our website: https://ajaysridhar.com/barx/.

</details>


### [117] [A Systems Engineering Framework for Vision-Language-Enabled UAV Triage and Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27597)
*Swapnil Saha,Bhuvan Rajanasiriyur Jagadeesha,Karishma Patnaik,Neelakshi Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索将视觉语言模型（VLM）作为人-UAV循环中的协调代理嵌入灾害响应系统，通过MBSE方法设计架构，整合自然语言交互、任务协调、软件在环实现及ICS通信协议，并包含VLM协调代理、无人机任务控制和任务分配三大核心组件。七名参与者的初步人因评估显示感知工作负荷（心理需求、努力、挫败感）降低，AI信任和通信清晰度评分较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在灾害响应中的应用主要局限于决策支持角色，如社交媒体监测、行动计划草拟和技术警报翻译等，这类方法增加了操作员负担，因为人类仍需将输出转化为跨团队和机器人资产的协调行动。本研究旨在探索VLM作为主动协调代理而非仅被动建议工具的可能性，以减轻操作员压力并提升人机协同效率。

Method: 采用Model-Based Systems Engineering（MBSE）方法开发框架，使用用例图和块定义图表示系统角色、内部结构和组件交互。实现了三个核心要素：VLM协调代理、无人机任务控制和任务分配器，并集成到模拟与控制环境中。框架包含自然语言交互、任务级协调、软件在环实现以及与事故指挥系统（ICS）对齐的通信协议。初步人因评估采用七名参与者进行感知工作负荷和AI信任度测试。

Result: 初步人因评估（七名参与者）显示：感知工作负荷在心理需求、努力和挫败感三个维度上有所降低；AI信任度和通信清晰度获得高评分，表明VLM作为协调代理具备可行性。

Conclusion: 通过整合MBSE、软件在环测试和人因评估，该工作推进了高风险灾害响应场景下的可扩展人-自主团队协作研究，对航空航天自主性和民用安全具有更广泛的应用启示。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Language Models (VLMs) have created new opportunities for disaster response, where responders must interpret large volumes of sensor data under time pressure. Current VLM applications include social media monitoring for situational awareness, generation of draft action plans, and translation of technical alerts into public-facing messages. While these efforts can accelerate information flow, they remain largely limited to decision-support roles. Such approaches can increase operator burden because humans must still translate outputs into coordinated actions across teams and robotic assets. This study explores the viability of embedding VLMs as coordination agents within the human-UAV loop. The proposed architecture integrates natural language interaction, mission-level task coordination, software-in-the-loop implementation, and communication aligned with the Incident Command System (ICS). Rather than functioning solely as advisory tools, VLMs facilitate communication between human operators, mission control logic, and UAV task execution. The framework was developed using a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) approach, with use case and block definition diagrams representing system roles, internal structure, and component interactions. Three key elements, the VLM Coordinator Agent, UAV Mission Control, and Task Allocator, were implemented within an integrated simulation and control environment. A preliminary human-factors evaluation with seven participants showed reduced perceived workload across mental demand, effort, and frustration, along with high ratings for AI trust and communication clarity. By integrating MBSE, software-in-the-loop testing, and human-factors evaluation, this work advances scalable human-autonomy teaming for high-stakes disaster response, with broader implications for aerospace autonomy and civil safety.

</details>


### [118] [Arm2Air: Cross-Embodiment Skeleton Transfer for 3D Relay Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27627)
*Dohun Lee,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Seokmin Kim,Byongho Lee,Seungjoo Oh,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Arm2Air提出了一种跨具身迁移框架，将机器人手臂的避障骨架知识迁移到无人机中继部署任务中，通过预训练Transformer迁移平台并结合低秩适应（LoRA）微调，在保证连接性、瓶颈容量、延迟和移动代价的同时，显著降低了规划运行时间和参数更新量，展现了高效的数据利用和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中无人机中继部署面临视距遮挡、通信范围、高度和三维障碍物等多重约束的联合优化难题，传统规划器计算代价高昂，且从零训练数据效率低下。受机器人手臂避障路径规划与无人机中继部署在有序结构推理上具有相似性的启发，作者希望探索跨具身迁移的可行性，以实现更高效的数据利用和计算开销。

Method: Arm2Air采用两阶段迁移学习框架：(1) 源域阶段，利用预训练的Neural MP模型生成的机器人手臂运动轨迹，转换为有序骨架序列，用于预训练基于Transformer的迁移平台；(2) 目标域阶段，使用少量无人机域目标数据和Low-Rank Adaptation（LoRA）进行适配微调。迁移得到的骨架用于初始化中继链，并通过优化连接性、瓶颈容量、延迟和移动代价进行精化。

Result: 在9个高杂波3D城市地图上，端到端规划运行时间中位数较最快传统规划器降低64.9%。在30个密集城市地图的高遮挡子集上，相比IMPC-MD，瓶颈容量提升32.6%，容量方差降低74.7%，最大跳距降低13.2%，跳距方差降低75.2%，中继位移降低16.9%。仅用3个目标域训练地图，相比从零训练，中继位置RMSE降低53.6%，仅更新13.4万参数（对比从零训练和全微调的138.3万）。

Conclusion: Arm2Air证明了跨具身迁移在无人机中继部署中的可行性和有效性，展示了计算和数据高效性，并提出了将有序结构先验跨异构具身任务迁移的更广泛原理。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) relay networks can restore connectivity after communication infrastructure is damaged. Urban relay placement is difficult because line-of-sight blockage, communication range, altitude, and three-dimensional obstacles must be considered jointly. Arm2Air transfers obstacle-avoidance skeletons from robot arms to UAV relay placement through cross-embodiment transfer. Source-domain robot-arm motions from a pretrained Neural MP model are converted into ordered skeletons that pretrain a transformer-based transfer platform, which is then adapted to the UAV domain using limited target data and Low-Rank Adaptation. The transferred skeleton initializes a relay chain that is refined for connectivity, bottleneck capacity, delay, and movement cost. On nine held-out high-clutter 3D urban maps, Arm2Air reduced median end-to-end planning runtime by 64.9 percent relative to the fastest conventional planner. On the high-obstruction group of a separate 30-map dense urban holdout, it increased bottleneck capacity by 32.6 percent, reduced capacity variance by 74.7 percent, reduced maximum hop distance by 13.2 percent, reduced hop-distance variance by 75.2 percent, and reduced relay displacement by 16.9 percent relative to IMPC-MD. With only three target-domain training maps, Arm2Air reduced relay-position root mean square error by 53.6 percent relative to training from scratch while updating 0.134 million parameters, compared with 1.383 million for Scratch and Full Fine-tuning. These results demonstrate computationally and data-efficient UAV relay placement and suggest a broader principle for transferring ordered structural priors across heterogeneous embodied tasks.

</details>


### [119] [LabEvolver: Training-Free Experience Evolution for Safe and Grounded Wet-Lab Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27690)
*Jingya Wang,Yuyang Gao,Liuzhenghao Lv,Yonghong Tian,Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LabEvolver是一个无需训练（training-free）的框架，为安全、有依据的湿实验室智能体赋予基于执行经验的"情景记忆"。它将状态接地（state-grounded）的内部试错循环（用于自适应感知、在线规划和安全验证）与外部演化循环（将完成的轨迹提炼为可复用的技能、策略和安全经验）相结合。在机器人溶液配制任务中，pH调节完成时间降低48.2%，安全门拦截降低60.0%；在ALFWorld基准上，20步内累计成功率从ReAct的76.2%提升至91.4%（跨500个连续任务），验证了该框架在湿实验室之外的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有湿实验室智能体缺乏在真实执行过程中积累和复用经验的能力，每次任务都需要从头规划，导致效率低下、安全验证成本高。作者希望探索一种"干中学"（learn-by-doing）的闭环节自动科学发现路径，使智能体能够从自身执行经验中持续演化，而不依赖昂贵的模型再训练。

Method: LabEvolver采用双循环架构：(1) 内部试错循环（inner trial loop）——基于状态接地（state-grounded）实现自适应感知、在线规划和实时安全验证；(2) 外部演化循环（outer evolution loop）——将已完成的执行轨迹蒸馏为三类可复用经验：技能经验（skill）、策略经验（strategy）和安全经验（safety），形成累积的情景记忆持续指导后续任务。

Result: 在机器人溶液配制任务中，pH调节完成时间减少48.2%，安全门拦截次数减少60.0%；在ALFWorld基准上，500个连续任务中20步内累计成功率从ReAct的76.2%提升至91.4%，验证了该方法在湿实验室之外的通用性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，通过执行经验驱动的经验演化（experience evolution）框架，无需训练即可显著提升湿实验室智能体的效率、安全性和任务成功率，支持"干中学"经验演化作为迈向闭环自动科学发现的一条可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce LabEvolver, a training-free framework that equips safe and grounded wet-lab agents with episodic memory from execution experience. LabEvolver couples a state-grounded inner trial loop for adaptive perception, online planning, and safety validation with an outer evolution loop that distills completed trajectories into reusable skill, strategy, and safety experience. On robotic solution-preparation tasks, LabEvolver demonstrates real-world feasibility, reducing pH-regulation completion time and safety-gate intercepts by 48.2% and 60.0%, respectively. On ALFWorld, it further improves cumulative success rate within 20 steps from 76.2% with ReAct to 91.4% over 500 continual tasks, showing generality beyond wet-lab settings. These results support learn-by-doing experience evolution as a feasible path toward closed-loop automated scientific discovery. The project page is available at https://github.com/AndyGao6186/LabEvolver.

</details>


### [120] [Write-Safe Flow Field Mapping under Ambiguous Onboard Sensing and Localization Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27713)
*Linhao Jin,Qimin Feng,Peter Gunnarson,Qiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"地图参考感知的保守融合框架"来解决移动机器人在流场建图中因观测模糊和定位漂移导致的重影结构污染问题。该方法通过预测局部速度补丁和学习到的写入安全分数，连续衰减不确定的地图更新，同时允许在没有可靠参考时进行初始化。在合成射流和横流环境中，平均重影污染降低42%；在真实推力器尾流的零样本硬件重放测试中，重影污染降低39%，同时保留81%的地图覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部流场结构的估计虽然看起来合理，但写入全局地图时并不总是安全的。相似的流场结构可能产生模糊观测，而定位漂移会导致预测补丁被写入错误位置，重复的错误注册更新会累积成持久性重影结构。现有方法未考虑此失效模式。

Method: 提出地图参考感知的保守融合框架：模型同时预测局部速度补丁和一个可学习的写入安全分数。该分数持续衰减不确定的地图更新，同时在没有可靠地图参考时允许初始化，实现不确定感知的自适应融合。

Result: 合成射流和横流环境中，平均重影污染相比无门控融合降低42%；零样本硬件重放（使用真实压力和光流测量）中重影污染进一步降低39%，同时保留81%的地图覆盖率。

Conclusion: 安全的地图写入对于在模糊感知和定位漂移条件下进行流场建图至关重要，所提出的保守融合框架有效缓解了重影结构问题。

Abstract: Mobile robots can infer local flow structure from onboard sensing, but a locally plausible estimate is not always safe to write into a global map. Similar flow structures may produce ambiguous observations, while localization drift causes predicted patches to be written at incorrect locations. Repeated misregistered updates then accumulate into persistent ghost structures. We address this failure mode with a map-reference-aware conservative fusion framework. The model predicts a local velocity patch and a learned write-safety score that continuously attenuates uncertain map updates while permitting initialization when no reliable map reference is available. Across synthetic jet and crossflow environments, the proposed method reduces average ghost contamination by 42% relative to ungated fusion. A zero-shot hardware replay using real pressure and optical-flow measurements from a thruster wake further reduces ghost contamination by 39% while retaining 81% map coverage. These results show that safe map writing is critical for flow mapping under ambiguous sensing and localization drift.

</details>


### [121] [RedFlow: Redirect Failure into Action-Level Corrections for Flow-matching VLA Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27782)
*Zhengyang Yan,Junhao Li,Fangqi Zhu,Zijun Wang,Quanxin Shou,Yikun Miao,Xiaoyi Pang,Zicong Hong,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RedFlow是一种针对流匹配视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的细粒度离线强化学习框架，通过将失败经验转化为动作级别的纠正式监督信号，实现从混合质量数据中的鲁棒恢复学习。在LIBERO基准和三个真实世界操作任务中，RedFlow优于SOTA离线RL基线，将真实世界成功率从56.7%提升到74.7%，并且以约一个数量级更少的训练样本匹敌如PPO等强在线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA策略在部署中常因分布偏移产生累积误差。尽管离线RL可利用回放数据改进部署策略，但现有方法要么忽略失败数据，要么仅在轨迹级别利用失败数据，导致学习效率低且错误持续存在。作者旨在通过细粒度地利用失败经验来解决这一问题。

Method: RedFlow包含两个核心组件：(1) Context-Aware Corrective Matching机制，识别引发失败的动作并从相似上下文中检索成功替代动作作为纠错目标；(2) Adaptive Redirection Objective，联合强化成功动作、抑制不良动作、并将可恢复失败重定向至纠错目标。该方法将成功和失败经验同时转化为密集监督，实现混合质量数据下的恢复学习。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和三个真实世界操作任务上，RedFlow一致优于SOTA离线RL基线，将真实世界成功率从56.7%提升至74.7%；同时以约一个数量级更少的训练样本匹敌PPO、GRPO和DDPO等强在线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将失败经验细粒度地转化为动作层面的纠错监督，RedFlow显著提升了离线RL框架下VLA策略的部署稳健性，实现了高效利用混合质量数据的学习，为离线策略优化提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Flow-matching Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation but often suffer from compounding errors caused by distribution shifts during deployment. While offline reinforcement learning (RL) provides a practical way to improve deployed policies using rollout data, existing methods either ignore failure data or exploit it only at the trajectory level, resulting in low learning efficiency and persistent errors. We propose **RedFlow**, a fine-grained offline RL framework that redirects failure experiences into action-level corrective supervision for flow-matching VLA policies. RedFlow consists of two key components: (1) a **Context-Aware Corrective Matching** mechanism that identifies failure-inducing actions and retrieves successful alternatives from similar contexts as corrective targets, and (2) an **Adaptive Redirection Objective** that jointly reinforces successful actions, suppresses undesirable ones, and redirects recoverable failures toward corrective targets. By converting both successful and failed experiences into dense supervision, RedFlow enables robust recovery learning from mixed-quality data. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark and three real-world manipulation tasks show that RedFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art offline RL baselines, improving the real-world success rate from 56.7% to 74.7%. It also matches strong on-policy methods (PPO, GRPO, and DDPO) while requiring roughly an order of magnitude fewer training samples.

</details>


### [122] [DexDirect: Direct Kinesthetic Arm Guidance for Efficient Dexterous Demonstration Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27784)
*Beom Jun Kim,Shiu-Jen Wang,Jonathan Liu,Alvin Zhu,Quanyou Wang,Hanzhang Fang,Feng Xu,Mingzhang Zhu,Yuchen Cui,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexDirect是一种直接的运动学手臂引导界面，用于高效收集灵巧操作示范。操作者通过手柄直接拖动6自由度重力补偿机械臂，同时单个网络摄像头将操作者的另一只手重定向到16关节13自由度的灵巧机械手上。相比纯视觉（AnyTeleop）和姿态跟踪（TeleDex）基线，DexDirect收集的成功示范数量分别多17.2倍和3.2倍，显著降低了心理需求、努力和挫败感，训练的扩散策略在立方体抓取放置任务中达到90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展地收集灵巧操作示范是机器人学习的主要瓶颈。高保真接口通常需要昂贵硬件和大量设置，而低成本低设置的替代方案控制精度较低且给操作者带来更大的认知负担。作者旨在设计一种既低成本低设置又高效精确的示范收集接口。

Method: DexDirect采用双通道方案：(1) 操作者通过手柄直接拖动6自由度重力补偿机械臂进行运动学引导（kinesthetic guidance），实现直观高效的手臂控制；(2) 通过单个网络摄像头对操作者另一只手进行重定向（retargeting），映射到16关节13自由度的灵巧机械手上。该系统无需昂贵的动作捕捉或专用手套硬件，设置简单。

Result: 用户研究表明DexDirect相比纯视觉基线（AnyTeleop）和姿态跟踪基线（TeleDex），收集的成功示范数量分别多17.2倍和3.2倍。改编的NASA-TLX评估显示，尽管物理需求增加，但心理需求、努力和挫败感显著降低。基于DexDirect示范训练的扩散策略在立方体抓取放置任务中达到90%成功率。

Conclusion: 直接将运动学手臂引导与基于视觉的手部重定向相结合，提供了一种低设置、可扩展且高效的灵巧操作示范收集界面，为机器人学习中的示范数据瓶颈问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Scalable collection of dexterous manipulation demonstrations remains a major bottleneck for robot learning. High-fidelity interfaces often require costly hardware and extensive setup, while low-setup, low cost alternatives tend to provide less precise control and impose greater cognitive workload on operators. We present DexDirect, a direct kinesthetic arm guidance for efficient dexterous demonstration collection. The operator drags a 6-DoF gravity-compensated robot arm directly by a handle, while a single webcam retargets operator's other hand onto a 16 joints 13-DoF dexterous robot hand. User studies suggest DexDirect collects 17.2x and 3.2x more successful demonstrations compared to purely vision (AnyTeleop) and pose-tracking (TeleDex) baselines. An adapted NASA-TLX shows DexDirect greatly reduces mental demand, effort, and frustration, despite raising physical demand. A diffusion policy trained on DexDirect demonstrations reaches a 90% success rate on a cube pick-and-place task. These results suggest that direct kinesthetic arm guidance combined with vision-based hand retargeting provides an efficient low-setup and scalable interface for collecting dexterous manipulation demonstrations

</details>


### [123] [RoboBRIDGE: A Modular Framework for Bridging Policies to Robust Real-World Robotic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27881)
*Sihyung Yoon,Minjong Yoo,Sanghyun Ahn,Seojeong Choi,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboBRIDGE是一个模块化框架，通过五个协调模块（Monitor、Perceptor、Planner、Controller、Robot Interface）将预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型系统性地转化为可靠的机器人智能体，解决了VLA模型缺乏故障恢复、长时程执行不一致以及鲁棒性不足等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽然作为动作预测器效果良好，但部署为机器人智能体时存在三个关键缺陷：缺乏故障恢复机制、长时程执行不一致、对观测/任务/本体（embodiment）变化的鲁棒性有限。现有的解决方案仅针对单个问题通过模型重训练或环境特定模块来修补，缺乏一个通用的系统性框架。

Method: RoboBRIDGE采用模块化编排层设计，包含五个协同模块：Monitor负责快速故障检测与分层恢复以防止错误级联；Planner在环境偏离当前计划时触发重新规划；Perceptor异步更新场景理解以避免执行停滞；Controller通过元技能微调将操作分解为域不变原语并配备专用的LoRA适配器，降低VLA使用时对域偏移的敏感性；Robot Interface负责与真实机器人平台的交互。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa基准以及跨多个机器人平台和VLA骨干网络的实际案例研究中，RoboBRIDGE一致地优于独立策略和先前增强型VLA部署方案。

Conclusion: 可靠的机器人智能体能力并非仅来自扩展动作预测器的规模，而来自围绕其进行的结构化编排。这一结果表明，VLA模型要成为真正可部署的机器人智能体，需要一个系统性的编排框架而非仅仅依赖模型本身的改进。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have attracted growing interest as a scalable approach to robotic manipulation. While these models are effective action predictors, deploying them as robotic agents exposes critical gaps: no mechanism for failure recovery, inconsistent execution over long horizons, and limited robustness to shifts in observations, tasks, or embodiments. Existing solutions address these limitations individually through model retraining or environment-specific modules, yet what is needed is a general framework that systematically transforms a pretrained VLA into a robotic agent. We present RoboBRIDGE, a modular framework that provides an orchestration layer over five coordinated modules, namely Monitor, Perceptor, Planner, Controller, and Robot Interface, to compose robust robotic agents from off-the-shelf components, including pretrained VLAs. The Monitor pairs rapid failure detection with hierarchical recovery to correct errors before they cascade. When the environment diverges from the current plan, the Planner triggers replanning while the Perceptor updates scene understanding asynchronously, avoiding execution stalls. Within the Controller, primitive skill fine-tuning factors manipulation into domain-invariant primitives with dedicated LoRA adapters, reducing sensitivity to domain shifts when a VLA is used. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world case studies spanning multiple robot platforms and VLA backbones, RoboBRIDGE consistently outperforms both standalone policies and prior augmented VLA deployments. These results suggest that reliable robotic agency does not arise from scaling action predictors alone, but from structured orchestration around them.

</details>


### [124] [Static In, Dynamic Out: Counterfactual Action Augmentation for Moving Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27890)
*Woo Chul Shin,Zhenyang Chen,Alfred Cueva,Nadun Ranawaka Arachchige,Yingyan Celine Lin,Benjamin Joffe,Shreyas Kousik,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SIDO是一种反事实动作增强方法，使仅基于静态物体演示训练的策略能够在测试时适应未见过的物体运动。其核心是将移动物体操作分解为"预测物体未来位置"和"到达预测位姿"两个子问题，通过位移物体到反事实未来位置并变形演示动作块以保持手-物相对位姿，最终形成目标条件策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉（visuomotor）策略在静态操作任务上表现良好，但实际部署中目标物体会移动（如传送带上零件漂移、风中果实摇摆），而现有方法无法处理这种动态场景。

Method: SIDO的核心思想是将移动物体操作分解为两个子问题：(1)预测物体将在哪里；(2)到达该预测位姿。具体做法是将物体移动到反事实的未来位置，并对演示的动作块进行变形以保持手-物体相对位姿，从而生成目标条件策略。部署时通过物体位姿预测器提供未来位置。

Result: 在三个模拟任务（Mug、Square、Stack）的5种物体运动模式下以及两个真实世界任务（Gantry、Peachtree）中，SIDO相比基线在移动物体任务上提升了成功率，同时保持静态物体任务性能不下降。

Conclusion: SIDO通过反事实动作增强和位姿分解，成功实现了仅从静态演示学习的策略对动态物体操作的泛化能力，兼顾了移动场景的适应性提升与静态场景的性能保持。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies have advanced on manipulation tasks where the target object stays static during execution, but real deployments break this assumption: parts drift on conveyors and fruits sway in the wind. We introduce Static In, Dynamic Out (SIDO), a counterfactual action augmentation that enables a policy trained only on static object demonstrations to adapt to unseen object motion at test time. Our key idea is to factorize moving object manipulation into two sub-problems: predicting where the object will be, and reaching that predicted pose. SIDO displaces the object to a counterfactual future position and morphs the demonstrated action chunk to preserve the hand-object relative pose, yielding a goal-conditioned policy. At deployment an object pose predictor supplies the future position. Across three simulated tasks (Mug, Square, Stack) under five object motion patterns and two real-world tasks (Gantry, Peachtree), SIDO improves moving object success over the baselines while preserving static object performance. Project website: https://sido-staticindynamicout.github.io/.

</details>


### [125] [Learning Social Robot Navigation By Sensing Human Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27922)
*Alberto Vaglio,Andrea Garulli,Antonio Giannitrapani,Renato Quartullo,Tommaso Van Der Meer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CALF（Convolutional Attention for Leg Features），一种端到端神经网络架构，直接从2D LiDAR扫描中解释腿部运动并生成安全导航指令，用于人群中的机器人导航。配套开发了轻量级2D模拟器LegNav（基于JAX，配有新型行人步态模型），通过深度强化学习训练策略，并在TurtleBot 4上实现零样本真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导航机器人在行人环境中通常使用贴近地面的2D LiDAR，但该高度的传感器主要检测运动的腿部而非完整人体。然而，大多数基于学习的导航方法仍将行人简化为圆形等简单形状，忽略了腿部运动信息，导致感知与建模之间存在差距。

Method: CALF采用卷积层、注意力机制和MLP的组合架构，端到端地直接从LiDAR扫描中解析腿部运动。策略通过深度强化学习在自建的LegNav模拟器中训练，该模拟器结合2D LiDAR射线追踪和一种新颖的行人步态模型，基于JAX实现以实现高效训练。

Result: CALF策略在导航性能和社会合规性方面优于经典及基于学习的基线方法。通过TurtleBot 4的零样本部署，在真实实验中产生了平滑且符合社会规范的轨迹。LegNav模拟器可在单张消费级GPU上于1小时内完成部署级策略的训练。

Conclusion: 利用腿部运动特征驱动的端到端学习策略，能够有效弥补低高度LiDAR感知与行人建模之间的差距，在仿真和真实环境中均实现安全、平滑且社会合规的导航，且训练效率高、部署成本低。

Abstract: Robots navigating among pedestrians typically sense their surroundings with a 2D LiDAR mounted close to the ground. At that height, the sensor mostly sees moving legs rather than whole people, yet most learning-based navigation methods still treat pedestrians as simple shapes like circles. This paper addresses that gap with CALF (Convolutional Attention for Leg Features), an end-to-end neural architecture that combines convolutional layers, attention, and MLP to interpret leg motion directly from LiDAR scans and produce safe navigation commands. The CALF policy is trained using deep reinforcement learning algorithms within LegNav, a custom lightweight 2D simulator that combines 2D LiDAR ray tracing with a novel pedestrian gait model. The resulting policy is compared against classical and learning-based baselines in terms of navigation performance and social compliance. The approach is validated through real-world experiments via zero-shot deployment on a TurtleBot 4, yielding smooth and socially compliant trajectories. Written in JAX, the LegNav simulator enables the training of a deployment-ready CALF policy in under an hour on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [126] [RaDiVe: Robust 4D Radar Odometry with Distance-Bounded NDT and Velocity-Discrepancy Point Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28045)
*Sangwoo Jung,Dongjae Lee,Chiyun Noh,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RaDiVe，一个4D雷达里程计框架，通过距离受限的NDT配准、基于速度不一致性的点不确定性加权模型，以及基于SDF隐式神经映射的表面点提取，显著提升4D雷达点云配准的精度和鲁棒性，在多个公开数据集上平均比现有基线降低44.4%的平移ATE和21.3%的旋转ATE，且保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D雷达在恶劣天气下具备鲁棒感知能力，但其点云固有的稀疏性、噪声和有限的位置精度，使得基于配准的里程计面临显著挑战，需要专门的框架来提升精度和稳健性。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 距离受限的NDT配准，限制对应搜索到近距离体素对以提升优化稳定性和计算效率；(2) 速度不一致性点不确定性模型，根据实测多普勒径向速度与由估计自车速度预测的径向速度之间的差异对每个4D雷达点进行加权；(3) 基于SDF隐式神经映射的表面点提取，构建几何一致且去噪的局部子地图。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的评估表明，RaDiVe相比现有4D雷达里程计基线，平均平移ATE降低44.4%，旋转ATE降低21.3%，同时保持实时运行性能。

Conclusion: RaDiVe通过结合距离受限NDT、速度一致性加权和SDF神经映射三种策略，有效解决了4D雷达点云稀疏、噪声和位置精度有限带来的配准难题，显著提升了里程计精度和鲁棒性，并将在开源社区发布源代码。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D radar enable robust perception in adverse weather; however, the inherent sparsity, noise, and limited positional precision of radar point clouds pose significant challenges for registration-based odometry. In this letter, we propose RaDiVe, a 4D radar odometry framework designed to improve the accuracy and robustness of radar point-cloud registration. We introduce a distance-bounded Normal Distributions Transform (NDT), which improves optimization stability and computational efficiency by restricting the correspondence search to near-distance voxel pairs. To mitigate measurement ambiguity, we propose a velocity-discrepancy point uncertainty model that weights each input 4D radar point according to the discrepancy between its measured Doppler radial velocity and the radial velocity predicted from the estimated ego-velocity. Furthermore, we incorporate Signed Distance Function (SDF)-based surface point extraction via implicit neural mapping to construct a geometrically consistent and noise-filtered local submap. Evaluations across multiple public datasets demonstrate that RaDiVe outperforms existing 4D radar odometry baselines by 44.4% in translational Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and 21.3% in rotational ATE on average, while maintaining real-time performance. The source code will be made publicly available to the robotics community: https://github.com/to-be-open-sourced.

</details>


### [127] [UniCross: Unified Cross-Skill Dexterous Manipulation Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28198)
*Hui Zhang,Julian Ferchow,Jie Song,Mirko Meboldt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的灵巧操作框架，将抓取、重定位、手内旋转和手内平移四种基本技能建模为单一公式，共享相同的状态/动作空间和目标结构，从而能够蒸馏出一个跨技能策略，在长时程操作中无缝链式组合这些技能，并跨手形态泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将灵巧操作的四种基础技能（抓取、重定位、手内旋转、手内平移）分别建模，各自采用特定的动作约束、目标和甚至专门的手部形态，破坏了长时程操作中所需的技能兼容性和连续性。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将四种技能建模在同一公式中，共享相同的状态空间和动作空间以及共同的目标结构；在该统一公式下训练单一跨技能策略，并支持策略蒸馏。

Result: 该框架在每种技能上均表现优异，能泛化到未见物体，对扰动具有鲁棒性，并能无缝地将技能链式组合成长时程操作；同时在不同手部形态之间有效迁移。

Conclusion: 不同灵巧操作技能可被视为共享任务公式的具体实例，揭示了不同行为之间内在的一致性，为长时程灵巧操作提供了统一的理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Many dexterous manipulation tasks require the object to remain securely held throughout the interaction. From the perspective of hand-object relational motion, such manipulation comprises four canonical skills: grasping, relocation, in-hand rotation, and in-hand translation. Human hands flexibly compose these skills to accomplish complex tasks. Existing approaches, however, model these skills separately with skill-specific action constraints, objectives, or even dedicated hand morphologies, which breaks the compatibility and continuity required for long-horizon composition. In this work, we present a unified framework that models all four skills in a single formulation that shares the same state and action spaces and a common objective structure. This formulation enables straightforward distillation of a single cross-skill policy that performs strongly on every skill, generalizes to unseen objects, stays robust to disturbances, and chains skills seamlessly into long-horizon manipulation. The framework also transfers effectively across different hand morphologies. Overall, our results suggest that different dexterous manipulation skills can be viewed as instantiations of a shared task formulation, revealing the intrinsic consistency across different behaviors.

</details>


### [128] [When Robots Exchange Meaning: A Demo of Goal-Oriented Semantic Communications for Collaborative Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28256)
*Peizheng Li,Xinyi Lin,Sajida Gufran,Adnan Aijaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个机器人-边缘语义通信（SemCom）测试平台，将机器人端视觉压缩、边缘端语义建图与仪表盘式任务交互集成于一体。通过VQ-VAE编码器将RGB图像压缩为token，在边缘端进行重建与RTAB-Map语义建图，实现了42.67倍的压缩比（320x240图像仅需5400字节），为6G场景下任务感知网络研究提供了实际验证平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人作为6G中典型的任务导向型用例，通信质量应体现在任务执行、环境理解和闭环操作等语义层面，而非仅以数据包投递衡量。现有研究缺乏将语义通信与机器人实际任务相结合的端到端测试平台，因此需要构建一个从视觉传输到目标级地图交互的完整演示系统。

Method: 系统采用分层的机器人-边缘架构：移动机器人配备RGB-D传感器与LiDAR，运行ROS 2并在机端通过ONNX Runtime执行VQ-VAE编码器将RGB帧转换为token；Jetson Orin边缘节点负责PyTorch解码器重建、RTAB-Map建图、语义物体处理以及基于浏览器的可视化；重建的视觉流与深度、位姿和3D建图信息关联，生成语义地图供下游机器人应用使用。

Result: 320x240的图像被表示为80x60的token网格，打包净荷为5400字节，相对模型输入RGB字节实现42.67倍的压缩比。系统成功演示了从语义视觉传输到目标级地图交互的完整路径。

Conclusion: 该测试平台为未来任务感知型6G网络研究提供了实用平台，连接了语义通信、具身AI和物理AI赋能的机器人技术三大交叉领域。

Abstract: Collaborative robotics is a representative task-oriented 6G use-case, where communication quality should be reflected in mission execution, environment understanding, and closed-loop operation rather than packet delivery alone. This demo paper presents a robot-edge semantic communication (SemCom) testbed integrating robot-side visual compression, edge-side semantic mapping, and dashboard-based mission interaction. A mobile robot equipped with RGB-D sensing and LiDAR runs ROS 2, while a Jetson Orin edge node performs reconstruction, RTAB-Map mapping, semantic object handling, and browserbased visualization. As an initial proof of concept, RGB frames are encoded on the robot into VQ-VAE tokens using an ONNX Runtime encoder and reconstructed on the edge using a PyTorch decoder. A 320 X 240 image is represented by an 80 X 60 token grid with a packed payload of 5400 bytes, corresponding to a 42.67X reduction relative to model-input RGB bytes. The reconstructed visual stream is further associated with depth, pose, and 3D mapping information to generate a semantic map for downstream robotic applications. The demo exposes the full path from semantic visual transport to object-level map interaction, and provides a practical platform for future task-aware 6G networking studies at the intersection of SemCom, embodied AI, and physical AI-enabled robotics. A video of the demo is available at https://tinyurl.com/Tos09

</details>


### [129] [SemAnCorr: Semantic Anchored Correspondence for Zero-Shot Manipulation Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28382)
*Xiaoxiang Dong,William Baron,Hongyi Chen,Uksang Yoo,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SemAnCorr是一种无需训练的框架，通过联合位姿-对应优化选择语义一致的锚点区域，并利用函数映射在物体表面传播约束，从而建立既保持语义一致性又具有几何连贯性的稠密对应关系，实现跨几何形状不同的物体实例的操作技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨物体实例（功能相似但几何不同）的操作技能迁移是机器人学习中的基础挑战。现有基于稠密视觉描述符和3D特征场的对应方法通过最近邻特征匹配，往往产生空间上不连贯的对应关系，无法恢复技能迁移所需的局部几何框架。

Method: 提出SemAnCorr框架，包含三个关键步骤：(1) 通过联合位姿-对应优化选择语义一致的锚点区域；(2) 利用函数映射在物体表面传播锚点约束；(3) 生成既保持语义一致性又具有几何连贯性的稠密对应关系。

Result: 在基于PartNet-Mobility构建的稠密对应基准上达到90.8%的语义准确率，在几何连贯性上超越近期最先进的基线方法。在真实世界实验中，仅使用单次演示即可实现显著更可靠的零样本操作技能迁移。

Conclusion: SemAnCorr验证了通过锚点选择与函数映射传播结合的方法能够有效解决跨几何异构物体的稠密语义对应问题，并且这种改进能直接转化为真实世界操作任务中更可靠的技能迁移性能。

Abstract: Transferring manipulation skills across object instances that share functionality but differ in geometry remains a fundamental challenge in robot learning. While recent correspondence methods leverage dense visual descriptors and 3D feature fields, nearest-neighbor feature matching often produces spatially incoherent correspondences that fail to recover the local geometric frames required for reliable skill transfer. We introduce SemAnCorr, a training-free framework that establishes dense correspondence by selecting semantically consistent anchor regions through joint pose-correspondence optimization and propagating these constraints over the object surface using functional maps. The resulting correspondences preserve both semantic consistency and geometric coherence, enabling object-centric manipulation skills to transfer across geometrically diverse instances. We evaluate SemAnCorr on a dense correspondence benchmark built on PartNet-Mobility, achieving 90.8% semantic accuracy in our benchmark evaluation while improving geometric coherence over recent state-of-the-art baselines. Finally, we show that these improvements translate directly into real-world manipulation performance: using a single demonstration, SemAnCorr enables substantially more reliable zero-shot manipulation skill transfer to previously unseen objects than existing correspondence methods. Videos and additional visualizations are available at [https://semancorr.github.io](https://semancorr.github.io) .

</details>


### [130] [TacWAM: Anchor-Guided World Action Model with Mechanics-Aware Tactile Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28391)
*Lei Jin,Yiding Ma,Xin Zhang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacWAM 是一种力学感知的触觉世界动作模型，通过空间对齐融合触觉编码器、触觉历史编码器和锚引导三模态注意力三个模块，将未来触觉状态预测与机器人动作生成相结合，在四个真实世界接触丰富操作任务中达到75.0%的平均成功率，超越最强基线37.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）主要依赖视觉未来预测，虽然能捕捉场景结构和物体运动，但对接触丰富操作中的力、形变、剪切和滑动等物理信息提供有限监督。这产生了两个设计需求：触觉未来应携带有意义的物理信息，同时不应成为动作生成的特权线索（privileged cues）。

Method: TacWAM采用三步策略：(1) 空间对齐融合（SAF）触觉编码器，将触觉外观、密集力场和形变流映射到共享潜预测空间，通过双边力和力矩重建保留全局接触信息；(2) 触觉历史编码器提供时间上下文，使未来触觉预测反映当前观测之外的力和形变变化；(3) 锚引导三模态（AGT）注意力将当前视觉/触觉锚、未来预测令牌和动作令牌分离，使未来触觉状态仅监督训练而动作分支无法直接读取。

Result: 在四个真实世界接触丰富操作任务（脆弱物体抓取、持续表面接触、动态手持操作）中，TacWAM平均成功率达75.0%，比最强基线高出37.5个百分点。分段消融实验显示，移除触觉历史会一致性地导致性能下降，放宽对未来预测目标的访问同样使性能退化。

Conclusion: 结果证明，当未来触觉监督与信息丰富的触觉表示以及部署一致性信息约束相结合时，可以有效改善接触感知的动作学习，验证了触觉模态在世界动作模型中的关键价值。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine future-state prediction with robot action generation, but existing approaches largely rely on visual futures. Visual prediction captures scene structure and object motion, yet provides limited supervision for force, deformation, shear, and slip during contact-rich manipulation. This creates two design requirements: tactile futures should carry meaningful physical information, and they should not become privileged cues for action generation. We present TacWAM, a mechanics-aware tactile WAM that addresses this challenge in three steps. First, a Spatially Aligned Fusion (SAF) Tactile Encoder maps tactile appearance, dense force fields, and deformation flow into a shared latent prediction space, with bilateral force and torque reconstruction preserving global contact information. Second, a tactile history encoder provides temporal context so future tactile prediction reflects how force and deformation change beyond the current tactile observation. Third, Anchor-Guided Tri-Modal (AGT) Attention separates current visual and tactile anchors, future prediction tokens, and action tokens, allowing future tactile states to supervise training without being directly read by the action branch. We evaluate TacWAM on four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks covering fragile grasping, sustained surface contact, and dynamic in-hand manipulation. TacWAM achieves an average success rate of 75.0%, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 37.5 percentage points. Staged ablations show consistent degradation when tactile history is removed and access to future prediction targets is relaxed. These results indicate that future tactile supervision can improve contact-aware action learning when combined with informative tactile representations and deployment-consistent information constraints.

</details>


### [131] [FasTac: A Curved Multispectral Vision-Based Tactile Sensor for High-Speed High-Precision 3D Shape and Force Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28416)
*Xiaofan Lu,Kaiji Huang,Jiahui Chen,Yuankai Lin,Hua Yang,Zhouping Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FasTac，一种曲面视觉触觉传感器，通过多光谱光度立体成像、边界先验快速泊松深度重建、位置感知动态卷积力估计（HyperForce）以及FPGA硬件加速，实现了紧凑形态下精确3D重建、三轴力估计和高速处理的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曲面视觉触觉传感器难以在紧凑形态下同时兼顾精确的三维重建、三轴力估计和高速度处理能力。曲面触觉指尖需要解析精细接触几何、区分法向与切向载荷并捕捉瞬态信号，而现有方案在这三方面存在明显短板。

Method: FasTac采用单图像传感器同时多光谱成像，获得空间对齐的多光谱观测用于鲁棒表面法向估计；随后通过边界先验加速泊松深度重建。力估计采用HyperForce，即位置感知动态卷积，建模曲面弹性体空间非均匀的力学响应并估计三轴力。完整的图像-法向-力流水线部署于FPGA上实现硬件加速。

Result: 近红外照明与边界先验将深度平均绝对误差（MAE）从0.2730 mm降至0.0415 mm；HyperForce在法向力和剪切力估计上分别取得2.74%和2.39%的归一化平均绝对误差（NMAE）；FPGA部署将处理延迟从GPU的3.26 ms缩短至1.09 ms。多物体重建、反馈抓取和振动测量验证了细粒度几何感知、稳定力反馈和动态接触感知能力。

Conclusion: FasTac证明了在紧凑曲面视觉触觉传感器中同时实现高精度三维重建、三轴力估计和毫秒级高速处理是可行的，为灵巧操作中的触觉感知提供了完整的软硬件协同解决方案。

Abstract: Curved tactile fingertips for dexterous manipulation must resolve fine contact geometry, distinguish normal and tangential loads, and capture transient signals. Existing curved vision-based tactile sensors struggle to combine accurate 3D reconstruction, three-axis force estimation, and high-speed processing in a compact form. This article presents FasTac, a curved vision-based tactile sensor integrating multispectral photometric stereo, dynamic-convolution force estimation, and hardware acceleration on a field-programmable gate array (FPGA). Single-image-sensor simultaneous multispectral imaging provides spatially aligned observations for robust surface normal estimation, followed by boundary-prior fast Poisson depth reconstruction. HyperForce uses position-aware dynamic convolution to model the spatially nonuniform mechanical response of curved elastomers and estimate three-axis forces. The complete image-to-normal-force pipeline is deployed on an FPGA. Experiments show that near-infrared (NIR) illumination and the boundary prior decrease depth mean absolute error (MAE) from 0.2730 mm to 0.0415 mm; HyperForce achieves normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) values of 2.74% and 2.39% for normal and shear forces, respectively; and FPGA deployment shortens processing latency from 3.26 ms on the GPU to 1.09 ms.
